Its derived statements go, through Keryx, to de Bruijn's planner. Not for Lean (debruijn), searching (searcher), or choosing what to work on next (poros). À utiliser pour préparer un EPIC `à planifier` — souvent le suivant, pendant que le précédent se livre. Ne tire pas la branche de l'EPIC et n'écrit aucun code. - **Equation-level tests for TIP / λ / belief coefficient; remove silent clamp**: The audit found: "Out of ~16 test files, exactly **one** asserts a thesis equation against a hand-computed value." Plus an opt-out for the silent [0.05, 0.95] clamp so the disjoint test can see TIP near 0. ([crbazevedo/learning-to-anticipate-flexible-choices](https://github.com/crbazevedo/learning-to-anticipate-flexible-choices/blob/HEAD/.dfg/agents/W1-4-equation-level-tests.md) · ★1)
